Simple and clear interface
No training required
InkLinka is a CRM for tattoo studios with one of the most intuitive interfaces on the market.
The team can start working from day one: requests, clients, projects, sessions, deposits and the calendar are organized in a clear logic, without unnecessary buttons or complicated training.
InkLinka was created together with tattoo artists, so the system works the way a studio actually operates: fewer clicks, more speed, and a comfortable experience on desktop, tablet and phone.
Try for free Any plan is available free for 14 days, including the full Expert version.
Project-based CRM for tattoo studios
Why tattoo studios choose InkLinka
InkLinka is built around the way tattoo studios actually work: every request becomes a structured project with the client, references, sessions, deposits, calendar events, communication history, and artist workflow connected in one place.

Lion and Rose Tattoo
A new project will be created and a session will be created automatically when the event is created
Tattoo studios do not run on appointments alone
Most studio software starts with a calendar. But tattoo work starts earlier: a client sends an idea, references, placement, size, style, availability, medical notes, budget questions, and deposit confirmation. If that information stays in DMs, the studio loses context before the session even begins.
Booking requests live in DMs
Client details, ideas and availability are buried in conversations.
References get lost
Images, screenshots and inspiration are scattered across chats and devices.
Deposits are disconnected
Payment status is often checked manually or tracked outside the booking.
Sessions lose context
Appointments appear in the calendar, but the project story is somewhere else.
The InkLinka difference
InkLinka connects the whole tattoo workflow
Instead of treating bookings, clients, files, deposits and sessions as separate pieces, InkLinka keeps them inside one project-based structure.
Every request becomes a project
A new booking request is not just a message. It becomes a structured tattoo project with client details, references, sessions, status and deposit visibility.
References stay where they belong
Reference images, notes and project details stay attached to the client and the tattoo project, not buried in old chats.
Sessions are connected to the project
Calendar events are not isolated appointments. They are part of the tattoo project history.
Deposits are visible in context
Deposit status is connected to the booking/project, so the team can quickly understand what is confirmed, pending or missing.
Client history is more than a profile
A client profile is useful, but InkLinka connects the client with projects, sessions, files, communication and payment context.
Built for studio + artists
InkLinka supports the studio workspace, artists, clients, sessions and operational visibility - not just one person's calendar.
Communication belongs to the project
Messages and important context should not live outside the workflow. InkLinka is designed to keep communication close to the project.
AI-ready by design
Because project, client, session, payment and communication data are structured, InkLinka can support smarter AI assistance for admins, artists and studio owners.
Not another generic CRM
InkLinka is designed for tattoo studios where the work is visual, personal, multi-session and project-based.

AI-ready workspace
AI helps where there is real work context
In InkLinka, AI supports specific studio tasks: website chat, processing requests, organizing project descriptions, generating work titles and preparing follow-up message drafts.
AI does not replace the team or make final decisions. It helps process incoming messages, organize information and identify the next step faster.
AI booking assistant
Designed to help studios respond faster to new requests, collect missing details and guide clients through the booking flow.
Project context summarization
Can support clear project briefs by summarizing long conversations, references and notes for the artist or manager.
Smart admin support
Can help identify missing deposits, incomplete client details, unconfirmed sessions or projects that need follow-up.
Client communication drafts
Generate polite, studio-specific replies while keeping final control with the studio team.
Artist workflow support
Prepare session context, reference summaries and project notes before the appointment.
Studio insights
Use structured data to help owners understand booking flow, no-shows, deposits, artist workload and operational bottlenecks.
Less chaos before the appointment. More clarity during the work.

Why not just use a calendar, spreadsheet or salon CRM?
Why not just a calendar? A calendar shows time. It does not hold the full tattoo project: client ideas, references, sessions, deposits, notes and communication history.
Why not spreadsheets? Spreadsheets can track data, but they do not create a connected client and project workflow for the team.
Why not Instagram DMs? DMs are useful for starting conversations, but they are not a reliable system for managing confirmed tattoo projects.
Why not generic CRM? Generic CRMs manage contacts and pipelines, but tattoo work is visual, session-based and artist-specific.
Why not salon software? Tattoo studios do not work like quick beauty appointments. Tattoo projects often involve references, consultation, deposits, multiple sessions and long-term client history.
InkLinka is built on a different product philosophy
0 1 Project first
Every tattoo starts as a project, not just an appointment.
0 2 Context stays together
Client, references, sessions, deposits and messages should not be separated.
0 3 Studio and artist both matter
The system should support the business workflow and the creative workflow.
0 4 Less admin, more clarity
The product should reduce follow-up, searching and manual coordination.
0 5 AI needs structure
AI becomes useful when the studio data is organized and connected.
0 6 Built for growth
A solo artist, small studio and multi-artist studio should be able to grow into the same system.
Trust
Built with tattoo studio workflows in mind
InkLinka is designed for studios moving beyond DMs and spreadsheets. No fake testimonials, no generic claims - just a product built around the operational reality of tattoo work.

Below you will find tattoo care recommendations — following these rules is important for proper healing and maintaining the quality of the work.\n\n1. FIRST HOURS\n\nIf the tattoo is covered with film (including second skin), do not remove it for the time recommended by the artist.\n\nIf the film is regular — remove it 2–4 hours after the session.\n\n2. HYGIENE\n\nAfter removing the film, gently wash the tattoo with warm water and mild antibacterial soap.\n\nPat dry with a disposable paper towel (do not rub).\n\nWash the tattoo 3–4 times a day for the first 5–7 days.\n\n3. MOISTURIZING\n\nApply a thin layer of recommended healing product 3–4 times a day.\n\nDo not use alcohol, hydrogen peroxide, petroleum jelly, or greasy creams.\n\n4. WATER\n\nShowers are allowed, but avoid prolonged steaming.\n\nBaths, pools, saunas, steam rooms, and open water are prohibited until complete healing (usually 3–4 weeks).\n\n5. CLOTHING\n\nWear clean, loose clothing made of natural fabrics.\n\nAvoid friction and pressure on the tattooed area.\n\n6. WHAT NOT TO DO\n\nDo not scratch or pick at scabs or peeling.\n\nDo not cover the tattoo with tight bandages unnecessarily.\n\nDo not apply makeup or sunscreen until fully healed.\n\n7. SUN\n\nAvoid direct sunlight until fully healed.\n\nAfter healing, use sunscreen SPF 50+ to preserve color.\n\n8. WHEN TO SEE A DOCTOR\n\nIf you notice increasing redness, severe swelling, pus discharge, sharp pain, or fever — consult a doctor.\n\nIf you have any questions about healing, please contact us — we are always ready to help.\n\nBest regards,\n{studio_name}\n{artist_name}69:T5e9,Hello, {client_name}!\n\nThank you for visiting our studio.
